155:["$","ul",null,{"children":["\n",["$","li",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Not the summary call."}]," The CLI appends its instruction as the last turn, and the hook\nreads it there: one of the two asks the CLI sends, from a ",["$","code",null,{"children":"user"}]," or ",["$","code",null,{"children":"system"}]," role, in a\nturn no longer than the instruction itself. 272:["$","p",null,{"children":["When the flicker is not passthrough, read the counter tmux keeps for it. Upstream\n",["$","a",null,{"href":"https://github.com/tmux/tmux/blob/3.4/tty.c#L210-L247","rel":"noreferrer noopener","target":"_blank","children":[["$","code",null,{"children":"tty.c"}]," at 3.4"]}]," drops the\nbytes it has queued for a client as soon as that client's own fd cannot drain about\neight screens of them (",["$","code",null,{"children":"tty_block_maybe"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"children":"TTY_BLOCK_START = 1 + sx*sy*8"}],"), and when\nthe queue later falls back below the stop threshold the same file forces a whole\nscreen redraw.
